10月 12 08:43:51 macmini-lab3 campaign-loop.sh[1877789]:     the performance/battery power states. Previously this
10月 12 08:43:51 macmini-lab3 campaign-loop.sh[1877789]:     was only done for AC, but is also needed for DC.
10月 12 08:43:51 macmini-lab3 campaign-loop.sh[1877789]:     
10月 12 08:43:51 macmini-lab3 campaign-loop.sh[1877789]:     This commit fixes the behaviour on some laptop GPUs,

10月 12 08:43:51 macmini-lab3 campaign-loop.sh[1877789]:     clock frequency, so the GPU was stuck on the lowest
